Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The intervals variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 707 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 351 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the intervals space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 707 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 351. QED.

Theorem 215.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For optimality condition pairs (A,B) in intervals theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 139 and the invariant I(A,B) = 500 mod 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 139 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the intervals structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 500. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
